Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in rooms theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 508 and the invariant I(A,B) = 730 mod 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to rooms theory, we decompose the problem into 508 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 730. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
